Football match predictions, from a model that shows its record

GoalIQ predicts any football match: win probability for each side, from a Dixon-Coles + machine-learning model. Pick two teams and the model does the rest. Free on the web and in the app. Expected goals and the top-10 scoreline table are part of GoalIQ Premium.

Predict a match on the web  Get it on Google Play Download on the App Store

No sign-up needed to try it. The app is a free download.

result accuracy across 137 completed matches, all competitions
Every prediction logged before kick-off and graded after, hits and misses included. See the full record →

What you get for every match

Win probability for home, draw and away. Expected goals for both sides. The most likely scorelines ranked by probability, plus head-to-head history and form.

The same model powers GoalIQ's free FPL tools: clean sheet probability, fixture difficulty, rate my team and price watch.

The model logs every prediction before kickoff. 54.7% correct results across 137 completed matches.

How GoalIQ predicts a match

Three taps from a fixture to a full statistical breakdown.

1

Pick the match

Choose any two teams from a covered league, the Champions League or an international tournament.

2

Let the model run

A Dixon-Coles and machine-learning xG ensemble, trained on thousands of real matches, computes the outcome in seconds.

3

See the full picture

Win probabilities, expected goals, likely scorelines, head-to-head history and each team's form trend.

Quick answers

The short version, straight from the source.

How accurate are GoalIQ football predictions?
GoalIQ publishes its full prediction track record: every prediction is logged before kick-off and graded against the final result afterwards, hits and misses included, with no edits. The current accuracy numbers are shown on this page and refreshed after every round, and the raw log is available as machine-readable JSON.
How does GoalIQ predict football matches?
GoalIQ uses a Dixon-Coles statistical model combined with a machine-learning expected-goals (xG) ensemble, trained on thousands of real club and international matches. For any fixture it estimates the win probability for each side and the draw, expected goals for both teams, and the probability of the most likely scorelines.
Is GoalIQ a betting app?
No. GoalIQ is an analytics tool, not a gambling service. It has no bookmaker links and gives no betting advice. Predictions are statistical estimates for fun, analysis and fantasy football planning.
